from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
R,G,B = a[:,:,0].astype(int), a[:,:,1].astype(int), a[:,:,2].astype(int)
# red mask: red high, green/blue low
red = (R>180)&(G<100)&(B<100)
print('red pixels', red.sum())
ys, xs = np.where(red)
print('x range', xs.min(), xs.max(), 'y range', ys.min(), ys.max())
# connected components of red
from scipy import ndimage
import scipy
lab, n = ndimage.label(red)
print('components', n)
sizes = ndimage.sum(red, lab, range(1,n+1))
# bounding boxes of components with size > 20
for i in range(1, n+1):
    if sizes[i-1] > 30:
        m = lab==i
        ys, xs = np.where(m)
        print(i, 'size', sizes[i-1], 'bbox x', xs.min(), xs.max(), 'y', ys.min(), ys.max(), 'centroid', xs.mean().round(1), ys.mean().round(1))

        # box: 4 points
        xs = [p[0] for p in box]; ys = [p[1] for p in box]
        print(round(min(xs)), round(min(ys)), text, round(float(score),2))
else:
    print('nothing found')

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage
from rapidocr_onnxruntime import RapidOCR

img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
R,G,B = a[:,:,0].astype(int), a[:,:,1].astype(int), a[:,:,2].astype(int)
red = (R>180)&(G<100)&(B<100)
lab, n = ndimage.label(red)
sizes = ndimage.sum(red, lab, range(1,n+1))
centers = {}
for i in range(1, n+1):
    if sizes[i-1] > 30:
        m = lab==i
        ys, xs = np.where(m)
        centers[(round(xs.mean(),1), round(ys.mean(),1))] = int(sizes[i-1])

# merge components closer than 12 px (markers may be split)
import itertools
pts = list(centers.keys())
merged = []
used = set()
for i,p in enumerate(pts):
    if i in used: continue
    cluster = [p]
    for j,q in enumerate(pts):
        if j>i and j not in used:
            if ((p[0]-q[0])**2+(p[1]-q[1])**2)**0.5 < 14:
                cluster.append(q); used.add(j)
    used.add(i)
    mx = np.mean([c[0] for c in cluster]); my = np.mean([c[1] for c in cluster])
    ms = sum(centers[c] for c in cluster)
    merged.append((mx, my, ms))
merged.sort(key=lambda t:t[1])
print('num markers:', len(merged))
for m in merged: print(round(m[0]), round(m[1]), m[2])
np.save('/home/user/markers.npy', np.array([[m[0],m[1]] for m in merged]))

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from rapidocr_onnxruntime import RapidOCR

img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
markers = np.load('/home/user/markers.npy')
ocr = RapidOCR()

def ocr_crop(crop):
    # try as-is
    res = []
    for scale in (3,4,5):
        c = crop.resize((crop.width*scale, crop.height*scale), Image.LANCZOS)
        r,_ = ocr(np.array(c))
        if r:
            for box, text, score in r:
                res.append((text, float(score)))
    return res

out = []
for k,(mx,my) in enumerate(markers):
    x0,y0 = int(mx)-32, int(my)-32
    x1,y1 = int(mx)+32, int(my)+32
    x0,y0 = max(0,x0), max(0,y0)
    x1,y1 = min(a.shape[1],x1), min(a.shape[0],y1)
    crop = img.crop((x0,y0,x1,y1))
    res = ocr_crop(crop)
    out.append((round(mx),round(my),res))

for mx,my,res in out:
    print(mx,my, res if res else 'NONE')

    x0,y0 = int(mx)-40, int(my)-40
    x1,y1 = int(mx)+40, int(my)+40
    crop = a[max(0,y0):y1, max(0,x0):x1]
    # colors histogram
    colors, counts = np.unique(crop.reshape(-1,3), axis=0, return_counts=True)
    order = np.argsort(-counts)
    top = [(colors[i].tolist(), int(counts[i])) for i in order[:6]]
    # white pixels (near 255), black pixels
    wh = ((crop>220).all(axis=2)).sum()
    bl = ((crop<60).all(axis=2)).sum()
    # non-background count: pixels far from background color 109,123,121
    bg = np.array([109,123,121])
    dist = np.abs(crop.astype(int)-bg).sum(axis=2)
    nonbg = (dist>60).sum()
    print(k, round(mx), round(my), 'white',wh,'black',bl,'nonbg',nonbg, 'topcolors', top)

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
markers = np.load('/home/user/markers.npy')

def ascii_of(mx, my, r=42, thresh=70):
    x0,y0 = int(mx)-r, int(my)-r
    x1,y1 = int(mx)+r, int(my)+r
    x0,y0 = max(0,x0), max(0,y0)
    x1,y1 = min(a.shape[1],x1), min(a.shape[0],y1)
    crop = a[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    dark = (crop.sum(axis=2) < 3*90)  # dark pixels
    # also red ring marker: (R>150)&(G<100)&(B<100)
    red = (crop[:,:,0]>150)&(crop[:,:,1]<100)&(crop[:,:,2]<100)
    H,W = dark.shape
    rows=[]
    for i in range(0,H,2):
        line=''
        for j in range(W):
            if dark[i,j]: line+='#'
            elif red[i,j]: line+='o'
            else: line+='.'
        rows.append(line)
    return '\n'.join(rows)

for idx in [0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8]:
    mx,my = markers[idx]
    print('='*70)
    print('marker', idx, (round(mx),round(my)))
    print(ascii_of(mx,my))

input.command:
cd /home/user && python3 << 'EOF'
from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
markers = np.load('/home/user/markers.npy')

def fine_ascii(mx, my):
    r=26
    x0,y0 = int(mx)-r, int(my)-r
    x1,y1 = int(mx)+r, int(my)+r
    crop = a[max(0,y0):y1, max(0,x0):x1]
    dark = (crop.sum(axis=2) < 3*95)
    # also very dark red (shadow) - exclude pure red
    H,W = dark.shape
    rows=[]
    for i in range(H):
        line=''
        for j in range(W):
            line += '#' if dark[i,j] else '.'
        rows.append(line)
    return '\n'.join(rows)

for idx in [0,1,2,3,4,5,6]:
    mx,my = markers[idx]
    print('#'*60)
    print('marker', idx, (round(mx),round(my)))
    art = fine_ascii(mx,my)
    print(art)
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
R,G,B = a[:,:,0].astype(int), a[:,:,1].astype(int), a[:,:,2].astype(int)
red = (R>180)&(G<100)&(B<100)
lab, n = ndimage.label(red)
sizes = ndimage.sum(red, lab, range(1,n+1))
# collect large components (markers)
comps=[]
for i in range(1,n+1):
    if sizes[i-1] > 150:
        m = lab==i
        ys, xs = np.where(m)
        comps.append((xs.mean(), ys.mean(), sizes[i-1]))
# merge close comps
merged=[]
used=set()
for i,(x,y,s) in enumerate(comps):
    if i in used: continue
    cl=[(x,y,s)]
    for j,(x2,y2,s2) in enumerate(comps):
        if j>i and j not in used and ((x-x2)**2+(y-y2)**2)**0.5<15:
            cl.append((x2,y2,s2)); used.add(j)
    used.add(i)
    mx=sum(c[0]*c[2] for c in cl)/sum(c[2] for c in cl)
    my=sum(c[1]*c[2] for c in cl)/sum(c[2] for c in cl)
    merged.append((mx,my))
merged.sort(key=lambda t:t[1])
print('markers:', len(merged))

def digit_ascii(mx,my,rad=20):
    # dark pixels inside circle radius rad
    out=[]
    for dy in range(-rad,rad+1):
        line=''
        for dx in range(-rad,rad+1):
            yy=int(my+dy); xx=int(mx+dx)
            if 0<=yy<a.shape[0] and 0<=xx<a.shape[1]:
                px=a[yy,xx]
                d = px.sum() < 3*95
                line += '#' if d else '.'
            else:
                line += ' '
        out.append(line)
    return '\n'.join(out)

for idx,(mx,my) in enumerate(merged):
    print('#'*50)
    print(idx, round(mx), round(my))
    print(digit_ascii(mx,my))
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage
from rapidocr_onnxruntime import RapidOCR

img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
R,G,B = a[:,:,0].astype(int), a[:,:,1].astype(int), a[:,:,2].astype(int)
red = (R>180)&(G<100)&(B<100)
lab, n = ndimage.label(red)
sizes = ndimage.sum(red, lab, range(1,n+1))
comps=[]
for i in range(1,n+1):
    if sizes[i-1] > 150:
        m = lab==i
        ys, xs = np.where(m)
        comps.append((xs.mean(), ys.mean(), sizes[i-1]))
merged=[]
used=set()
for i,(x,y,s) in enumerate(comps):
    if i in used: continue
    cl=[(x,y,s)]
    for j,(x2,y2,s2) in enumerate(comps):
        if j>i and j not in used and ((x-x2)**2+(y-y2)**2)**0.5<15:
            cl.append((x2,y2,s2)); used.add(j)
    used.add(i)
    mx=sum(c[0]*c[2] for c in cl)/sum(c[2] for c in cl)
    my=sum(c[1]*c[2] for c in cl)/sum(c[2] for c in cl)
    merged.append((mx,my))
merged.sort(key=lambda t:t[1])
print('markers:', len(merged))

ocr = RapidOCR()

def clean_digit_img(mx,my,rad=16):
    # build 2*rad+1 image, dark px inside circle -> black, else white
    arr = np.full((2*rad+1,2*rad+1,3), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    for dy in range(-rad,rad+1):
        for dx in range(-rad,rad+1):
            yy=int(round(my+dy)); xx=int(round(mx+dx))
            if 0<=yy<a.shape[0] and 0<=xx<a.shape[1]:
                px=a[yy,xx]
                if px.sum() < 3*100:
                    arr[dy+rad,dx+rad]=[0,0,0]
    im = Image.fromarray(arr)
    return im

results=[]
for idx,(mx,my) in enumerate(merged):
    im = clean_digit_img(mx,my)
    big = im.resize((im.width*8, im.height*8), Image.LANCZOS)
    arr = np.array(big.convert('L'))
    # binarize
    arr = (arr<128).astype(np.uint8)*255
    r,_ = ocr(arr)
    txt = ''
    if r:
        # join texts sorted by x
        items = sorted(r, key=lambda t: min(p[0] for p in t[0]))
        txt = ''.join(t[1] for t in items)
    results.append((idx,round(mx),round(my),txt))
    print(idx, round(mx), round(my), repr(txt))

np.save('/home/user/markers_final.npy', np.array([[m[0],m[1]] for m in merged]))
import json
json.dump(results, open('/home/user/ocr_results.json','w'))
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
R,G,B = a[:,:,0].astype(int), a[:,:,1].astype(int), a[:,:,2].astype(int)

def scene(x0,y0,x1,y1):
    crop = a[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    out=[]
    for i in range(0,crop.shape[0],2):
        line=''
        for j in range(crop.shape[1]):
            r,g,b = crop[i,j]
            if (r>180 and g<100 and b<100): line+='o'
            elif (r+g+b < 3*100): line+='#'
            else: line+='.'
        out.append(line)
    return '\n'.join(out)

# area around markers 3 (310,214), 4 (427,216), 5 (379,222)
print('=== REGION x200-470 y185-255 ===')
print(scene(200,185,470,255))
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
R,G,B = a[:,:,0].astype(int), a[:,:,1].astype(int), a[:,:,2].astype(int)
red = (R>180)&(G<100)&(B<100)
lab, n = ndimage.label(red)
sizes = ndimage.sum(red, lab, range(1,n+1))
# collect all ring components with bbox
rings=[]
for i in range(1,n+1):
    if sizes[i-1] > 150:
        m = lab==i
        ys, xs = np.where(m)
        rings.append({'x':xs.mean(),'y':ys.mean(),'s':sizes[i-1],
                      'x0':xs.min(),'x1':xs.max(),'y0':ys.min(),'y1':ys.max(),
                      'r': (xs.max()-xs.min())/2.0})
# find rings near markers 3,4,5 (indices 3,4,5 of sorted-by-y list)
def rings_near(x,y,tol=30):
    return [r for r in rings if ((r['x']-x)**2+(r['y']-y)**2)**0.5<tol]
targets=[(310,214),(427,216),(379,222)]
for (x,y) in targets:
    nr = rings_near(x,y)
    print('target',x,y,'-> rings',[(round(r['x']),round(r['y']),round(r['r'])) for r in nr])
# list ALL rings in y order to map them to markers
rings.sort(key=lambda r:r['y'])
print('\nAll rings (x,y,r):')
for r in rings:
    print(round(r['x']), round(r['y']), round(r['r']))
np.save('/home/user/rings.npy', np.array([[r['x'],r['y'],r['r']] for r in rings]))
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
rings = np.load('/home/user/rings.npy')

def digit_ascii(cx,cy,r,factor=0.72):
    R = max(4,int(r*factor))
    out=[]
    for dy in range(-R,R+1):
        line=''
        for dx in range(-R,R+1):
            yy=int(round(cy+dy)); xx=int(round(cx+dx))
            if 0<=yy<a.shape[0] and 0<=xx<a.shape[1]:
                px=a[yy,xx]
                dark = px.sum() < 3*95
                line += '#' if dark else '.'
            else:
                line += '?'
        out.append(line)
    return '\n'.join(out)

for idx,(cx,cy,r) in enumerate(rings):
    print('='*46)
    print(f'#{idx} ({round(cx)},{round(cy)}) r={r}')
    print(digit_ascii(cx,cy,r))
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage
from rapidocr_onnxruntime import RapidOCR
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
rings = np.load('/home/user/rings.npy')
ocr = RapidOCR()

def crop_digit(cx,cy,r,pad=6):
    R = int(r)+pad
    x0,y0 = max(0,int(cx)-R), max(0,int(cy)-R)
    x1,y1 = min(a.shape[1],int(cx)+R), min(a.shape[0],int(cy)+R)
    crop = a[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    # dark -> black on white
    dark = crop.sum(axis=2) < 3*95
    arr = np.full((crop.shape[0],crop.shape[1],3),255,np.uint8)
    arr[dark]=0
    return Image.fromarray(arr)

for idx,(cx,cy,r) in enumerate(rings):
    if idx in (18,21,28) or idx in (4,5):
        im = crop_digit(cx,cy,r)
        big = im.resize((im.width*10, im.height*10), Image.NEAREST)
        arr = np.array(big.convert('L'))
        rr,_ = ocr(arr)
        txt=''
        if rr:
            items = sorted(rr, key=lambda t: min(p[0] for p in t[0]))
            txt = ''.join(t[1] for t in items)
        # also try LANCZOS
        big2 = im.resize((im.width*10, im.height*10), Image.LANCZOS)
        arr2 = np.array(big2.convert('L'))
        arr2 = (arr2<200).astype(np.uint8)*255
        rr2,_ = ocr(arr2)
        txt2=''
        if rr2:
            items = sorted(rr2, key=lambda t: min(p[0] for p in t[0]))
            txt2 = ''.join(t[1] for t in items)
        print(f'ring {idx} ({round(cx)},{round(cy)}) r={r} OCR_nearest={txt!r} OCR_lanczos={txt2!r}')
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
rings = np.load('/home/user/rings.npy')

def ascii(cx,cy,R):
    out=[]
    for dy in range(-R,R+1):
        line=''
        for dx in range(-R,R+1):
            yy=int(round(cy+dy)); xx=int(round(cx+dx))
            if 0<=yy<a.shape[0] and 0<=xx<a.shape[1]:
                px=a[yy,xx]
                line += '#' if px.sum()<3*95 else '.'
            else: line+='?'
        out.append(line)
    return '\n'.join(out)

for idx in (18,21,28):
    cx,cy,r = rings[idx]
    print('='*50)
    print(f'ring {idx} ({round(cx)},{round(cy)}) r={r}, window ±{int(r)+8}')
    print(ascii(cx,cy,int(r)+8))
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from rapidocr_onnxruntime import RapidOCR
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
rings = np.load('/home/user/rings.npy')
ocr = RapidOCR()

def tight_digit_img(cx,cy,r,pad=4,thresh=285):
    R = int(r)+pad
    x0,y0 = max(0,int(cx)-R), max(0,int(cy)-R)
    x1,y1 = min(a.shape[1],int(cx)+R), min(a.shape[0],int(cy)+R)
    crop = a[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    dark = crop.sum(axis=2) < thresh
    if dark.sum()==0: return None
    ys,xs = np.where(dark)
    x0b,x1b,y0b,y1b = xs.min(),xs.max(),ys.min(),ys.max()
    # pad
    x0b=max(0,x0b-2); y0b=max(0,y0b-2)
    x1b=min(crop.shape[1],x1b+3); y1b=min(crop.shape[0],y1b+3)
    sub = dark[y0b:y1b, x0b:x1b]
    arr = np.full((sub.shape[0],sub.shape[1]),255,np.uint8)
    arr[sub]=0
    return Image.fromarray(arr)

results={}
for idx,(cx,cy,r) in enumerate(rings):
    im = tight_digit_img(cx,cy,r)
    if im is None:
        results[idx]=''
        continue
    best=''
    for scale in (12,16):
        big = im.resize((im.width*scale, im.height*scale), Image.NEAREST)
        res,_ = ocr(np.array(big))
        if res:
            items = sorted(res, key=lambda t: min(p[0] for p in t[0]))
            best = ''.join(t[1] for t in items)
            break
    results[idx]=best

for idx in range(len(rings)):
    print(idx, round(rings[idx][0]), round(rings[idx][1]), repr(results[idx]))
import json; json.dump(results, open('/home/user/ocr2.json','w'))

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
rings = np.load('/home/user/rings.npy')

def extract(cx,cy,r,pad=6,thresh=285):
    R = int(r)+pad
    x0,y0 = max(0,int(cx)-R), max(0,int(cy)-R)
    x1,y1 = min(a.shape[1],int(cx)+R), min(a.shape[0],int(cy)+R)
    crop = a[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    dark = crop.sum(axis=2) < thresh
    if dark.sum()==0: return None
    ys,xs = np.where(dark)
    x0b,x1b,y0b,y1b = xs.min(),xs.max(),ys.min(),ys.max()
    x0b=max(0,x0b-1); y0b=max(0,y0b-1)
    x1b=min(crop.shape[1],x1b+2); y1b=min(crop.shape[0],y1b+2)
    return dark[y0b:y1b, x0b:x1b].astype(float)

def split_digits(binimg):
    # split into digits by column gaps
    colsum = binimg.sum(axis=0)
    cols = [i for i in range(len(colsum))]
    # find gaps: columns with sum==0
    groups=[]
    cur=[]
    for i,s in enumerate(colsum):
        if s>0: cur.append(i)
        else:
            if cur: groups.append(cur); cur=[]
    if cur: groups.append(cur)
    # merge groups separated by tiny gaps (<1px) -> unlikely; keep all
    return groups

# known digit templates: manual readings
known = {}
# (ring_idx, digit_position) -> digit char
refs = {
 (0,):'1', (2,):'2', (3,):'4', (4,):'6', (1,):'5', (6,):'8', (7,):'7', (8,):'9',
}
# ring 19 = '25', 20='24', 21='20', 22='22', 23='27',24='29',25='23',26='26',27='28',
# 28='31',29='30',30='33',31='32',32='34',33='35',34='36',35='37',36='38',37='39'
multi = {19:'25',20:'24',21:'20',22:'22',23:'27',24:'29',25:'23',26:'26',27:'28',
         28:'31',29:'30',30:'33',31:'32',32:'34',33:'35',34:'36',35:'37',36:'38',37:'39'}

templates={}  # digit char -> list of normalized binary imgs
for idx,ch in refs.items():
    im = extract(*rings[idx])
    if im is not None:
        templates.setdefault(ch,[]).append(im)
for idx,ch in multi.items():
    im = extract(*rings[idx])
    if im is None: continue
    groups = split_digits(im)
    if len(groups)!=len(ch):
        print('split issue ring',idx,'want',ch,'groups',len(groups))
        continue
    for g,digit in zip(groups,ch):
        sub = im[:, g[0]:g[-1]+1]
        templates.setdefault(digit,[]).append(sub)

def norm(im):
    # pad to fixed 24x30 canvas centered
    H,W = im.shape
    canvas = np.zeros((30,24))
    h0 = (30-H)//2; w0=(24-W)//2
    canvas[h0:h0+H, w0:w0+W]=im
    return canvas

templates_n = {d:[norm(t) for t in lst] for d,lst in templates.items()}
print('template counts:', {d:len(l) for d,l in templates_n.items()})

def match(im):
    n = norm(im)
    best=None; bestscore=-1
    for d,lst in templates_n.items():
        for t in lst:
            score = (n*t).sum()/max(1,(n.sum()+t.sum()))
            if score>bestscore:
                bestscore=score; best=d
    return best, bestscore

# ambiguous rings
for idx in [18,21,4,5,28,13,14,15,16,17,9,10,11,12]:
    im = extract(*rings[idx])
    if im is None:
        print(idx,'None'); continue
    groups = split_digits(im)
    digits=[match(im[:,g[0]:g[-1]+1]) for g in groups]
    print(f'ring {idx} ({round(rings[idx][0])},{round(rings[idx][1])}) -> ' + ''.join(d for d,s in digits))

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
rings = np.load('/home/user/rings.npy')

def black_ascii(cx,cy,R,thresh=150):
    out=[]
    for dy in range(-R,R+1):
        line=''
        for dx in range(-R,R+1):
            yy=int(round(cy+dy)); xx=int(round(cx+dx))
            if 0<=yy<a.shape[0] and 0<=xx<a.shape[1]:
                px=a[yy,xx]
                line += '#' if px.sum()<thresh else '.'
            else: line+='?'
        out.append(line)
    return '\n'.join(out)

for idx in [18,21,4,5]:
    cx,cy,r = rings[idx]
    print('='*42)
    print(f'ring {idx} ({round(cx)},{round(cy)}) r={r}')
    print(black_ascii(cx,cy,14))

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
R,G,B = a[:,:,0].astype(int), a[:,:,1].astype(int), a[:,:,2].astype(int)
red = (R>180)&(G<100)&(B<100)
lab, n = ndimage.label(red)
sizes = ndimage.sum(red, lab, range(1,n+1))
comps=[]
for i in range(1,n+1):
    if sizes[i-1] > 60:
        m = lab==i
        ys, xs = np.where(m)
        comps.append((xs.mean(), ys.mean(), sizes[i-1], xs.min(),xs.max(),ys.min(),ys.max()))
# cluster by proximity <10
clusters=[]
for c in sorted(comps, key=lambda t:t[1]):
    placed=False
    for cl in clusters:
        if ((c[0]-cl[0][0])**2+(c[1]-cl[0][1])**2)**0.5 < 12:
            cl.append(c); placed=True; break
    if not placed:
        clusters.append([c])
print('clusters:', len(clusters))
for cl in clusters:
    xs=[c[0] for c in cl]; ys=[c[1] for c in cl]
    print(round(np.mean(xs)), round(np.mean(ys)), 'parts', len(cl))
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
rings = np.load('/home/user/rings.npy')

def black_ascii(cx,cy,R=15,thresh=150):
    rows=[]
    for dy in range(-R,R+1):
        line=''
        for dx in range(-R,R+1):
            yy=int(round(cy+dy)); xx=int(round(cx+dx))
            if 0<=yy<a.shape[0] and 0<=xx<a.shape[1]:
                px=a[yy,xx]
                line += '#' if px.sum()<thresh else '.'
            else: line+='?'
        rows.append(line)
    return rows

allrows=[]
for idx,(cx,cy,r) in enumerate(rings):
    allrows.append((idx, round(cx), round(cy), black_ascii(cx,cy)))

# print 2 per row group for compactness: ring i and ring i+1 side by side
def side(a,b):
    out=[]
    for ra,rb in zip(a,b):
        out.append(ra + '   |   ' + rb)
    return '\n'.join(out)

for i in range(0,38,2):
    if i+1<38:
        print('#'*40, 'ring',allrows[i][0],allrows[i][1:3], 'vs ring', allrows[i+1][0],allrows[i+1][1:3])
        print(side(allrows[i][3], allrows[i+1][3]))
    else:
        print('ring', allrows[i][0], allrows[i][1:3])
        print('\n'.join(allrows[i][3]))
    print()
EOF

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img).astype(int)
R,G,B = a[:,:,0], a[:,:,1], a[:,:,2]
# find saturated non-gray colors (max-min > 60) that are NOT the red ring color (237,28,36)
sat = (a.max(axis=2) - a.min(axis=2))
nonred_sat = (sat>60) & ~((R>180)&(G<100)&(B<100))
print('non-red saturated pixels:', nonred_sat.sum())
ys,xs = np.where(nonred_sat)
if len(ys):
    print('x', xs.min(), xs.max(), 'y', ys.min(), ys.max())
    # cluster
    coords = np.column_stack([xs,ys])
    from scipy.spatial import cKDTree
    # just count unique colors
    cols = a[nonred_sat]
    u,counts = np.unique(cols, axis=0, return_counts=True)
    order=np.argsort(-counts)
    for i in order[:20]:
        print(u[i], counts[i])

from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
a = np.array(img)
# blue marker: B high, G mid, R low
blue = (a[:,:,2]>150)&(a[:,:,0]<100)&(a[:,:,1]>100)&(a[:,:,1]<220)
print('blue pixels', blue.sum())
if blue.sum():
    lab, n = ndimage.label(blue)
    for i in range(1,n+1):
        m=lab==i
        ys,xs=np.where(m)
        if len(ys)>5:
            print('component', i, 'center', round(xs.mean()), round(ys.mean()), 'size', len(ys), 'bbox', xs.min(),xs.max(),ys.min(),ys.max())
    # ascii around blue area
    ys,xs=np.where(blue)
    cx,cy = xs.mean(), ys.mean()
    print('ascii around blue center', round(cx), round(cy))
    x0,y0=int(cx)-30,int(cy)-30
    crop=a[y0:y0+60,x0:x0+60]
    for i in range(0,60,2):
        line=''
        for j in range(60):
            r,g,b = crop[i,j]
            if (b>150 and r<100): line+='B'
            elif (r+g+b<300): line+='#'
            elif (r>180 and g<100): line+='o'
            else: line+='.'
        print(line)

import numpy as np, random, math, itertools

towns = {
 1:('Bark',821,133), 2:('Fishing Village',701,186), 3:('Sho-Battai',379,222),
 4:('Burn\'s Tower',310,214), 5:('Flotsam Village',426,155), 6:('World\'s End',427,216),
 7:('Stoat',636,269), 8:('Heft',755,268), 9:('Settled Nomad Village',120,352),
 10:('Mongrel',224,386), 11:('Blister Hill',381,365), 12:('Tengu\'s Vault',611,368),
 13:('Heng/Trader\'s Edge',684,369), 14:('Bad Teeth',394,447), 15:('Stack',319,471),
 16:('Black Desert City',543,472), 17:('Waystation 1',619,480), 18:('Eyesocket',690,482),
 19:('Dust King Tower',387,516), 20:('Waystation 2',402,554), 21:('The Hub',336,528),
 22:('Smugglers Bar',514,569), 23:('Tower of Abuse',597,580), 24:('Brink',729,539),
 25:('Black Scratch',811,532), 26:('Crab Village',962,586), 27:('Admag',201,571),
 28:('Waystation 3',263,602), 29:('Squin',295,576), 30:('Last Stand',173,675),
 31:('Mud Town',363,657), 32:('Shark',350,686), 33:('Flats Lagoon',583,684),
 34:('Mourn',555,746), 35:('Catun',491,803), 36:('Slave Markets',407,810),
 37:('Clownsteady',298,838), 38:('Waystation 4',279,872), 39:('Drifter\'s Last',234,922),
}
IDS = list(towns)
POS = {i: np.array(towns[i][1:], float) for i in IDS}
D = {i:{j: float(np.linalg.norm(POS[i]-POS[j])) for j in IDS} for i in IDS}

START = 21  # The Hub

def tour_len(t):
    return sum(D[t[k]][t[k+1]] for k in range(len(t)-1))

def two_opt(t):
    n=len(t); improved=True; best=tour_len(t)
    while improved:
        improved=False
        for i in range(1,n-1):
            for j in range(i+1,n):
                if j-i==1: continue
                nt = t[:i]+t[i:j+1][::-1]+t[j+1:]
                nl = tour_len(nt)
                if nl < best-1e-9:
                    t=nt; best=nl; improved=True
    return t, best

def three_opt(t):
    # basic 3-opt: try or-opt moves (relocate segment)
    n=len(t); best=tour_len(t)
    for _ in range(3):  # passes
        changed=False
        for i in range(1,n-2):
            for j in range(i+1,n-1):
                for k in range(j+1,n):
                    # try reversing segment i..j
                    nt = t[:i]+t[i:j+1][::-1]+t[j+1:]
                    nl=tour_len(nt)
                    if nl<best-1e-9: t=nt; best=nl; changed=True; break
                if changed: break
            if changed: break
        if not changed: break
    return t,best

def nearest_neighbor(start):
    un=set(IDS)-{start}; t=[start]; cur=start
    while un:
        nxt=min(un, key=lambda x: D[cur][x])
        t.append(nxt); un.remove(nxt); cur=nxt
    return t

def cheapest_insertion(start):
    t=[start]; un=set(IDS)-{start}
    # start with nearest to start
    first=min(un, key=lambda x: D[start][x])
    t.append(first); un.remove(first)
    while un:
        # pick node with min insertion cost
        best_cost=1e18; best_node=None; best_pos=None
        for node in un:
            for p in range(len(t)-1):
                c = D[t[p]][node]+D[node][t[p+1]]-D[t[p]][t[p+1]]
                if c<best_cost:
                    best_cost=c; best_node=node; best_pos=p+1
        t.insert(best_pos,best_node); un.remove(best_node)
    return t

def farthest_insertion(start):
    t=[start]; un=set(IDS)-{start}
    first=max(un, key=lambda x: D[start][x])
    t.append(first); un.remove(first)
    while un:
        # farthest node from tour
        best_dist=-1; best_node=None
        for node in un:
            d=min(D[node][p] for p in t)
            if d>best_dist: best_dist=d; best_node=node
        best_cost=1e18; best_pos=None
        for p in range(len(t)-1):
            c=D[t[p]][best_node]+D[best_node][t[p+1]]-D[t[p]][t[p+1]]
            if c<best_cost: best_cost=c; best_pos=p+1
        t.insert(best_pos,best_node); un.remove(best_node)
    return t

# constructive candidates
candidates=[]
candidates.append(('NN', nearest_neighbor(START)))
candidates.append(('CI', cheapest_insertion(START)))
candidates.append(('FI', farthest_insertion(START)))
# random NN restarts
random.seed(42)
for k in range(200):
    # random start order -> NN from start with random tie-break
    un=set(IDS)-{START}; t=[START]; cur=START
    while un:
        dists=[(D[cur][x],x) for x in un]
        dists.sort()
        # pick among 3 nearest randomly
        pool=[x for _,x in dists[:3]]
        nxt=random.choice(pool)
        t.append(nxt); un.remove(nxt); cur=nxt
    candidates.append((f'RND{k}',t))

best_t=None; best_l=1e18; best_name=''
for name,t in candidates:
    t,l = two_opt(list(t))
    t,l = three_opt(t)
    if l<best_l:
        best_l=l; best_t=t; best_name=name
print('best from:', best_name, 'length', round(best_l,1))

# post-optimize: run 2-opt again with different initial? already done
path=best_t
print('Path (town numbers):', ' -> '.join(str(i) for i in path))
print('Path (names):')
for i in path:
    print(f'  {i:2d} {towns[i][0]}')

# verify all visited
assert sorted(path)==sorted(IDS)
# save
np.save('/home/user/path.npy', np.array(path))

 31:('Mud Town',363,657), 32:('Shark',350,686), 33:('Flats Lagoon',583,684),
 34:('Mourn',555,746), 35:('Catun',491,803), 36:('Slave Markets',407,810),
 37:('Clownsteady',298,838), 38:('Waystation 4',279,872), 39:('Drifter\'s Last',234,922),
}
IDS=sorted(towns); POS={i:np.array(towns[i][1:],float) for i in IDS}
D={i:{j:float(np.linalg.norm(POS[i]-POS[j])) for j in IDS} for i in IDS}
START=21

def tour_len(t): return sum(D[t[k]][t[k+1]] for k in range(len(t)-1))

def two_opt(t):
    n=len(t); best=tour_len(t); improved=True
    while improved:
        improved=False
        for i in range(1,n-1):
            for j in range(i+1,n):
                if j-i<2: continue
                a,b,c,d = t[i-1],t[i],t[j-1],t[j] if j<n else t[-1]
                delta = D[t[i-1]][t[j]]+D[t[j-1]][t[i]]-D[t[i-1]][t[i]]-D[t[j-1]][(t[j] if j<n else t[-1])]
                if delta < -1e-9:
                    t = t[:i]+t[i:j][::-1]+t[j:]
                    best += delta; improved=True
    return t, best

def double_bridge(t):
    n=len(t)
    if n<8: return t
    a=random.randint(1,n//3); b=random.randint(a+1,2*n//3); c=random.randint(b+1,n-1)
    t = t[:a]+t[b:c]+t[a:b]+t[c:]
    return t

path=np.load('/home/user/path.npy').tolist()
best_t,best_l = two_opt(path)
random.seed(7)
for it in range(400):
    cand = double_bridge(list(best_t))
    cand,l = two_opt(cand)
    if l<best_l-1e-9:
        best_l=l; best_t=cand
print('length', round(best_l,1))
path=best_t
print('Path:', ' -> '.join(str(i) for i in path))
for i in path:
    print(f'  {i:2d} {towns[i][0]}')
np.save('/home/user/path2.npy', np.array(path))

def tour_len(t): return sum(D[t[k]][t[k+1]] for k in range(len(t)-1))

def two_opt(t):
    n=len(t); improved=True
    while improved:
        improved=False
        for i in range(1,n-1):
            for j in range(i+1,n):
                if j-i<2: continue
                delta = D[t[i-1]][t[j]]+D[t[i]][t[j-1]]-D[t[i-1]][t[i]]-D[t[j-1]][t[j]]
                if delta < -1e-9:
                    t = t[:i]+t[i:j][::-1]+t[j:]
                    improved=True
    return t

def double_bridge(t):
    n=len(t); a=random.randint(1,n//3); b=random.randint(a+1,2*n//3); c=random.randint(b+1,n-1)
    return t[:a]+t[b:c]+t[a:b]+t[c:]

def tour_len(t): return sum(D[t[k]][t[k+1]] for k in range(len(t)-1))

def two_opt(t):
    n=len(t); improved=True
    while improved:
        improved=False
        for i in range(1,n-1):
            for j in range(i+1,n):
                # reverse t[i:j+1]

def tour_len(t): return sum(D[t[k]][t[k+1]] for k in range(len(t)-1))

def two_opt(t):
    n=len(t)
    while True:
        best_delta=0; best_ij=None
        for i in range(1,n-1):
            for j in range(i+1,n):
                # reversing t[i..j] (inclusive)
                d = D[t[i-1]][t[j]] + D[t[i]][t[j-1]] - D[t[i-1]][t[i]] - D[t[j-1]][t[j]]
                if d < best_delta - 1e-12:
                    best_delta=d; best_ij=(i,j)
        if best_ij is None: break
        i,j=best_ij
        t = t[:i] + t[i:j+1][::-1] + t[j+1:]
    return t

def double_bridge(t):
    n=len(t); a=random.randint(1,max(1,n//3)); b=random.randint(a+1,max(a+1,2*n//3)); c=random.randint(b+1,n-1)
    return t[:a]+t[b:c]+t[a:b]+t[c:]

D={i:{j:float(np.linalg.norm(POS[i]-POS[j])) for j in IDS} for i in IDS}

def tour_len(t): return sum(D[t[k]][t[k+1]] for k in range(len(t)-1))

def two_opt(t, cap=200):
    n=len(t)
    for _ in range(cap):
        best_l=tour_len(t); best_t=None
        for i in range(1,n-1):
            for j in range(i+1,n):
                nt = t[:i]+t[i:j+1][::-1]+t[j+1:]
                l = tour_len(nt)
                if l < best_l-1e-9:
                    best_l=l; best_t=nt
        if best_t is None: break
        t=best_t
    return t

def double_bridge(t):
    n=len(t); a=random.randint(1,max(1,n//3)); b=random.randint(a+1,max(a+1,2*n//3)); c=random.randint(b+1,n-1)
    return t[:a]+t[b:c]+t[a:b]+t[c:]

path=np.load('/home/user/path.npy').tolist()
best_t=two_opt(list(path)); best_l=tour_len(best_t)
print('after 2-opt:', round(best_l,1), flush=True)
random.seed(7)
for it in range(200):
    cand = two_opt(double_bridge(list(best_t)), cap=100)
    l = tour_len(cand)
    if l<best_l-1e-9:
        best_l=l; best_t=cand
        print('improved', round(best_l,1), flush=True)
print('FINAL length', round(best_l,1))
print('Path:', ' -> '.join(str(i) for i in best_t))

START=21
def tour_len(t): return sum(D[t[k]][t[k+1]] for k in range(len(t)-1))
def two_opt(t, cap=200):
    n=len(t)
    for _ in range(cap):
        best_l=tour_len(t); best_t=None
        for i in range(1,n-1):
            for j in range(i+1,n):
                nt = t[:i]+t[i:j+1][::-1]+t[j+1:]
                l = tour_len(nt)
                if l < best_l-1e-9: best_l=l; best_t=nt
        if best_t is None: break
        t=best_t
    return t
def double_bridge(t):
    n=len(t); a=random.randint(1,max(1,n//3)); b=random.randint(a+1,max(a+1,2*n//3)); c=random.randint(b+1,n-1)
    return t[:a]+t[b:c]+t[a:b]+t[c:]

best_t=None; best_l=1e18
for trial in range(400):
    un=set(IDS)-{START}; t=[START]; cur=START
    while un:
        dists=sorted((D[cur][x],x) for x in un)
        nxt=rng.choice([x for _,x in dists[:3]])
        t.append(nxt); un.remove(nxt); cur=nxt
    t=two_opt(t, cap=100)
    l=tour_len(t)
    if l<best_l-1e-9: best_l=l; best_t=t

 10:('Mongrel',224,386), 11:('Blister Hill',381,365), 12:('Tengu\'s Vault',611,368),
 13:('Heng/Trader\'s Edge',684,369), 14:('Bad Teeth',394,447), 15:('Stack',319,471),
 16:('Black Desert City',543,472), 17:('Waystation 1',619,480), 18:('Eyesocket',690,482),
 19:('Dust King Tower',387,516), 20:('Waystation 2',402,554), 21:('The Hub',336,528),
 22:('Smugglers Bar',514,569), 23:('Tower of Abuse',597,580), 24:('Brink',729,539),
 25:('Black Scratch',811,532), 26:('Crab Village',962,586), 27:('Admag',201,571),
 28:('Waystation 3',263,602), 29:('Squin',295,576), 30:('Last Stand',173,675),
 31:('Mud Town',363,657), 32:('Shark',350,686), 33:('Flats Lagoon',583,684),
 34:('Mourn',555,746), 35:('Catun',491,803), 36:('Slave Markets',407,810),
 37:('Clownsteady',298,838), 38:('Waystation 4',279,872), 39:('Drifter\'s Last',234,922),
}
path=np.load('/home/user/path_best.npy').tolist()

def segs_cross(p1,p2,p3,p4):
    def orient(a,b,c):
        return (b[0]-a[0])*(c[1]-a[1])-(b[1]-a[1])*(c[0]-a[0])
    o1=orient(p1,p2,p3); o2=orient(p1,p2,p4); o3=orient(p3,p4,p1); o4=orient(p3,p4,p2)
    return (o1*o2<0) and (o3*o4<0)

edges=[]
for k in range(len(path)-1):
    a=towns[path[k]][1:]; b=towns[path[k+1]][1:]
    edges.append((a,b))
crossings=[]
for i in range(len(edges)):
    for j in range(i+1,len(edges)):
        if abs(i-j)==1: continue
        if segs_cross(edges[i][0],edges[i][1],edges[j][0],edges[j][1]):
            crossings.append((i,j))
print('crossings:', crossings if crossings else 'NONE')

# Render on the original map
img = Image.open('/home/user/uploads/SmallMarked.png').convert('RGB')
d = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
pts = [(towns[n][1], towns[n][2]) for n in path]
# draw path segments with arrowheads
col = (255, 200, 0)
for k in range(len(pts)-1):
    d.line([pts[k], pts[k+1]], fill=col, width=3)
# markers
for n,(name,x,y) in towns.items():
    r=8
    c = (0,162,232) if n==21 else (237,28,36)
    d.ellipse([x-r,y-r,x+r,y+r], outline=c, width=3)
# numbers
try:
    font = ImageFont.truetype("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf", 14)
except:
    font = ImageFont.load_default()
for n,(name,x,y) in towns.items():
    d.text((x+12,y-8), str(n), fill=(255,255,255), font=font)
# start marker label
d.text((towns[21][1]+12, towns[21][2]+12), "START", fill=(0,162,232), font=font)
img.save('/home/user/route_map.png')
print('saved route_map.png')

}
UC = [n for n,(nm,f) in towns.items() if f=='UC']

def resolve(locs):
    best=None; bpos=10**9
    for x in locs:
        if x=='UC':
            for u in UC:
                if pos[u]<bpos: bpos=pos[u]; best=u
        else:
            if pos[x]<bpos: bpos=pos[x]; best=x
    return best

# List 1: the 7 mechanic-bound characters
list1 = {}
for c in ['Griffin','Bard','Ruka','Kang','Riddly','Jewel','Izumi']:
    list1[c]=resolve(chars[c])

# List 2: all characters
